3SHA Test Bench

One fixed test plan, applied identically to every instrument. Settings are ranked by whether they survive across independent time periods — a high return that only works once ranks below a modest one that keeps working.

generated 2026-07-23 00:59 UTC

1 · Test plan

SHA smoothing (base + HTF1)2,4 · 3,6 · 4,8 · 6,11 · 8,16 · 10,20How much the trend layers are smoothed. Smaller reacts sooner but whipsaws more; larger is calmer but later.Exit trigger (alignment break)base flip · HTF1 flip · HTF2 flipWhich timeframe turning against the trade closes it. Base is tightest, HTF2 loosest.Initial stop anchorSwing · HTF1 Body · HTF2 BodyWhat the stop is placed behind -- recent structure, or the mid/high timeframe trend body.Anchor basisBody · WickBody uses the candle body edge (tighter). Wick uses the full high/low including the shadow (wider).Stop stylemental · hardMental only triggers when a candle CLOSES beyond the stop. Hard is a real order that fires intra-bar and survives gaps.Risk per trade0.5% · 1.0% · 2.0%Percent of the account risked from entry to stop. Scales return and drawdown together.
648 combinations per strategy, per instrument  ·  every instrument gets the identical grid
EntryFull Alignment, Confirmed (1 bar) -- longs and shortsHTF2 smoothing2,2 (held fixed)Break-evenon -- stop moves to entry at +1RTrailing stopon -- swing/6 bars, same basis, starts at +1R, close-basedPartial take-profitoffGap skipoffEquity cap100% (so risk% is the only sizing lever)Stop buffer0.05% of median price (scale-relative, so it means the same on any instrument)Cost0.1% per round tripValidationfull history + 5 equal time blocks; ranked on block consistency, not raw return

4 · Run

  1. Upload the data in chat and name the instrument. Any number of timeframe files — 4h, daily and weekly are the minimum this plan needs.
  2. Files are stored in the repo under data/<INSTRUMENT>/ so they never need re-uploading.
  3. The full plan runs — 648 combinations per strategy — and every setting is checked across 5 independent time blocks.
  4. This page regenerates with a new instrument section: top 3 settings, plus the full grid underneath.
This page is the record, not the machine — it can't run the engine itself. Saying "run <instrument>" in chat is what starts a test.
